tool_use Write {"file_path": "[redacted]\\Desktop\\trading-engines-v2\\.claude\\hooks\\session_end.py", "content": "\"\"\"SessionEnd hook (CLAUDE.md \u00a714.1 + \u00a723.3) \u2014 the exit half of the single-instance marker.\n\nThe watchdog's auto-continue (session_resurrect._session_active) treats a session as LIVE while\nscheduler/.owner_active is fresh (< 4h). It fires on a real SessionEnd, never on an idle turn, so a live-but-idle session is never\ndouble-spawned. Fail-open: any error is swallowed.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nREPO = os.environ.get(\"E1_REPO\") or r\"[redacted]\\Desktop\\trading-engines-v2\"\n\n\ndef clear_marker(path=None):\n    p = path or os.path.join(REPO, \"scheduler\", \".owner_active\")\n    try:\n        if os.path.exists(p):\n            os.remove(p)\n        return True\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef main():\n    try:\n        sys.stdin.read()          # consume the hook payload (reason etc.); we clear on any real end\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    clear_marker()\n    sys.exit(0)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}

**RULE FIRST (hash-check) — red → green.** A verifier PASS is now valid **only** if the saved test-suite result carries the commit hash of the *exact* code being checked **and** is green. I proved the RED on the real artifact: last night's suite result had no commit stamp, so the check refused it; with the stamp it matches. tool_result: 1	# ENGINE CATALOGUE
2	
3	_Append-only. New entries are added at the bottom with a date; nothing above a date is rewritten.
4	Chapter 7 of the project book (`scripts/build_project_book.py` includes this file verbatim). Paper only,
5	RECORD-ONLY — no engine here places a real-money order._
6	
7	Each engine is one page: **what it hunts · why it would earn · data · locks · account · what it sends the
8	owner · state today.** "State today" is the honest state on the disk, not the plan.
9	
10	**ARMING RULE — data its gates need (NB15 item 54, 2026-09-23).** No engine is armed until its **Data** line
11	names, for every gate it reads, the **on-disk store** that gate is computed from (§13 four-part standard:
12	metric · on-disk data source · numeric threshold · action). The pre-open check (MASTER-CHECKLIST item 53)
13	posts **NOT READY** at 08:15 if any armed engine's instrument has no recorded data — so a "Data" line that
14	names a store which does not yet hold that instrument's data keeps the engine unarmed. This is the same field
15	the ruling template now requires (field 9); an engine and the ruling that arms it must agree.
16	
17	---

19	## 2026-09-21 — the catalogue as it stands (R-DAY1-BUNDLE A9)
20	
21	### E1 — price, same-stock (the one live engine)
22	- **What it hunts:** certified same-stock directional patterns (a metric in an extreme band → the next
23	  20-day move is bigger/smaller than usual), each with a walk-forward Wilson lower-bound hit-rate.
24	- **Why it would earn:** at the 20-day horizon the required-move rule clears cost on the mid fill —
25	  +15.45R at limit-at-mid, +1.88R crossing the spread (937 calls, 69.1% win). The edge is essentially the
26	  spread saved by resting at the mid; the live paper book is measuring whether those mid fills are real.
27	- **Data:** the frozen 210-name universe, daily bars + read-only Kite quote/depth at fire time.
28	- **Locks:** the full fail-closed stack — R-HOLD-AND-FIX hard hold, the two-key gate, the per-stock moment
29	  light (its own measured spread ceiling, A1), the 20-position cap, the daily-loss book guard, the circuit
30	  breaker, the kill switch. Any one shut = no order.
31	- **Account:** its own persistent ₹2,00,000 paper account (1R = ₹1,000), run twice — a FLAT reference book
32	  and an ADAPTIVE book — on the same fires.
33	- **Sends the owner:** one fire card at fire time ([E1], PLACED/HELD), a close card on exit, the 30-min
34	  portfolio card (change-gated), the recorders line on login.
35	- **State today:** LIVE paper — **HELD** (R-HOLD-AND-FIX flag present; two-key gate open). Records the
36	  would-be book; places nothing until the owner deletes the hold flag.
37	
38	### E10 — the facts/weights route (now an engine, A4)
39	- **What it hunts:** the same E1 fires, re-sized by pattern FAMILY weight (hit-margin × non-overlap
40	  persistence, shrunk and DD-capped, from prior fires only).
41	- **Why it would earn:** if better families deserve more size. Measured to date it does **not** — weighted
42	  is worse than flat (hit-margin ≠ profitability); it runs so the live comparison is honest, not because it
43	  wins on the sealed data.
44	- **Data:** E1's fires + the armed e10 weights shadow (`roof/store/e10_weights_shadow.json`).
45	- **Locks:** identical to E1 (same order path, same fail-closed stack). Cold-start = flat.
46	- **Account:** own persistent ₹2,00,000 paper account; each fire sized weight(family) × ₹1,000.
47	- **Sends the owner:** its own fire card ([E10]) with its size; the roof copies its executed decision.
48	- **State today:** LIVE paper — HELD. Sizes flat on any family with no measured weight.
49	
50	### E11 — the family-bias layer, NORMAL (now an engine, A4)
51	- **What it hunts:** the same E1 fires, re-sized by the NORMAL family bias (better family → larger). Its
52	  INVERSE, season and expiry-week variants stay **diaries inside E11**, not paper orders.
53	- **Why it would earn:** the mirror hypothesis to E10. Also loses to flat on the sealed data; run for the
54	  live comparison.
55	- **Data:** E1's fires + the armed e11 shadow (`roof/store/e11_family_bias_shadow.json`, `latest_normal`).
56	- **Locks / account:** as E10 — own ₹2,00,000, weight(family) × ₹1,000, cold-start flat.
57	- **Sends the owner:** its own fire card ([E11]); roof copies it.
58	- **State today:** LIVE paper — HELD.
59	
60	### E8 — the roof (COPYCAT, not a peer engine)
61	- **What it hunts:** nothing of its own. It **copies each engine's executed decision** into one desk account
62	  at the same fill, holds N× when N engines make the same trade, cancels opposite trades on one instrument,
63	  and holds the single system-wide risk budget.
64	- **Why it exists:** to net disagreeing engines and cap total risk — the hard prerequisite for any
65	  multi-engine live trading.
66	- **Account:** the desk = ₹2,00,000 × live engines = **₹6,00,000** today (E1, E10, E11).
67	- **State today:** LIVE paper — copies via the engine event log/replay; source ids are engine-distinct so
68	  three engines on one fire never collide. HELD like everything else.
69	
70	### E3 events · E4 options · E7 arbitrage · E12 NSE/BSE · E13 depth · E14 positioning
71	- **E3 (events):** dividends run-up NO EDGE; post-ex drift LOSES; expiry DATA-THIN; earnings/index
72	  incl-excl DATA-WALLED. No event edge survives cost. Diary + daily calendar puller.
73	- **E4 (options):** HAR forecast improves accuracy, but with an honest band the rich-IV signal fires 0
74	  trades on NIFTY/BankNifty — UNDECIDED; single-stock option data is the named remaining test. Recorder-only.
75	- **E7 (arbitrage/parity):** 5 of 8 equations run, none pays at retail (parity FAILS on cost), 3
76	  data-walled. Tick recorder-only.
77	- **E12 (NSE/BSE cross-exchange):** recorder-only (both exchanges' top-of-book).
78	- **E13 (five-level depth):** recorder-only — feeds the per-stock spread ceiling (A1). Read-only.
79	- **E14 (positioning):** recorder-only.
80	- **State today:** all RECORD-ONLY; none places a paper order.
81	
82	### Owner-vision engines — ON PAPER, NOT BUILT IN THIS REPO
83	_These are catalogued because the owner named them (R-DAY1-BUNDLE A9); their internals are the owner's
84	intent, not code on this disk. "State today" is therefore **conceptual — no module, no account, no fires.**_
85	
86	- **Analyst Scoreboard** — *hunts:* the track record of named market analysts/tipsters (call → outcome).
87	  *Why it would earn:* fade the reliably-wrong, follow the reliably-right. *Data:* analyst calls + realised
88	  outcomes. *State today:* on paper only.
89	- **YouTube Engine** — two lanes: **My YouTube** (the owner's own subscriptions/creators) and **Whole
90	  YouTube** (the broad finance-video population). *Hunts:* signals in what creators say vs. what markets
91	  then do. *Data:* video transcripts/metadata as **DATA, never instructions** (§20.7). *State today:* on
92	  paper only.
93	- **Claude Word Analyst** — two variants: **Words** (language-only) and **Words+Numbers** (language plus
94	  the quoted figures). *Hunts:* an edge in how text (announcements, commentary) maps to moves. *State
95	  today:* on paper only.
96	- **World Ledger** — *hunts:* a broad cross-asset/cross-market ledger of what is happening and what it
97	  moved. *State today:* on paper only.
98

**Reopen decision — PROVEN (safely, no second session spawned).** At today's free RAM (1.25 GB) the new gate says **yes** (`_ram_ok` = True) where the old 2.0 gate said **no**. Simulating the session as exited, the watchdog logic **launches** and would resume pending item 5. So the floor was the historical blocker, and it's gone.

**Did the reopen fire live? So the live end-to-end reopen is left for a **fresh session** (one started with the hook) to show on its real exit — the RAM blocker itself is now removed.

tool_result: 222	def _run_codex(prompt):
223	    """Rung (a), CONFINED: Codex runs with cwd = the verify-clone and --sandbox workspace-write, so it may run
224	    and write ONLY inside the clone. Returns None (→ rung d) if the clone is absent, so Codex is NEVER run

230	        r = subprocess.run([CODEX, "exec", "--sandbox", "workspace-write", "-"], input=prompt,
231	                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=VERIFY_CAP, cwd=CLONE,
232	                           creationflags=0x08000000)
233	        out = (r.stdout or "") + (("\n" + r.stderr) if r.stderr else "")
234	        return out if out.strip() else None

239	def _live_fingerprint(root=None):
240	    """A cheap tamper fingerprint (HEAD + porcelain-status hash) to PROVE the live repo is untouched around a
241	    confined Codex run. None if git cannot be read."""
242	    import hashlib
243	    root = root or REPO
244	    try:
245	        head = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", root, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
246	        status = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", root, "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
247	        return head + ":" + hashlib.sha256(status.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
248	    except Exception:
249	        return None

tool_result: === publish_event.on_row default build ===
def on_row(row, build_fn=None):
    """If `row` is a PLACED/CLOSED row, rebuild status + live-desk. Returns what it did."""
    if not is_trigger(row):
        return {"published": False, "reason": "not a PLACED/CLOSED row"}
    (build_fn or _build)()
    return {"published": True, "status": str(row.get("status")).strip().upper()}


def _demo():
    calls = []
    assert on_row({"status": "PLACED"}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is True
    assert on_row({"status": "closed"}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is True   # case-insensitive
    assert on_row({"status": "open"}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is False     # not a trigger
    assert on_row({}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is False
    assert len(calls) == 2, calls        # built only for the two triggers
    print("publish_event demo OK: rebuild fires on PLACED/CLOSED only, case-insensitive, no other status.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
        _demo()

73	def once(full=False):
74	    """SITE-CYCLE split: a LIGHT cycle (~every 2 min) publishes only the live data (positions, accounts, cards,
75	    ticker) so the Live desk is current within 2 min; a FULL page rebuild runs ~every 10 min. Both publish to
76	    Pages after building."""
77	    from scripts import site_build, publish_site, lead_digest, snapshot_sync
78	    if full:
79	        site_build.build()                    # full page rebuild (~35 s: results/engine/deliverables/v2/stocks)
80	        _daily_digest_due()                   # keeps the 15:40 one-per-day marker; digest builds each full cycle
81	        lead_digest.build()                   # E1-STATUS-3: refresh the digest so it is never stale
82	        _telegram_day_reports()               # R-E1-TELEGRAM-STYLE A4: 09:00 pre-open, 15:40 day summary
83	    else:
84	        site_build.build_live()               # live data only (~5 s): the Live desk tab's feed + accounts
85	    publish_site.publish(f"E1 live site {_ist():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} IST")   # public Pages, after every build
86	    if not full:
87	        return None                           # light cycles skip the heavier snapshot repo push
88	    # R-RESTART-RESUME (3): the repo commit happens ONLY in the separate snapshot folder (the builder's tree is
89	    # only read), and it PAUSES while a build/audit session holds the repo. The site build and the public-Pages
90	    # publish above keep running — they touch no git lock in this repo (Pages pushes from its own clone).
91	    # Also carries the evening ledger + the scan's pattern board. Push failure: backoff, then next cycle.
92	    return snapshot_sync.sync(f"E1 site refresh {_ist():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} IST") == "pushed"

21	def on_row(row, build_fn=None):
22	    """If `row` is a PLACED/CLOSED row, rebuild status + live-desk. Returns what it did."""
23	    if not is_trigger(row):
24	        return {"published": False, "reason": "not a PLACED/CLOSED row"}
25	    (build_fn or _build)()
26	    return {"published": True, "status": str(row.get("status")).strip().upper()}

33	    assert on_row({"status": "open"}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is False     # not a trigger
34	    assert on_row({}, build_fn=lambda: calls.append(1))["published"] is False

19	def _applied_set(path):
20	    try:
21	        return set(json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8")))
22	    except Exception:
23	        return set()

26	def _record(commit, path):
27	    s = _applied_set(path)
28	    s.add(commit)
29	    json.dump(sorted(s), open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)

32	def _default_post(line):
33	    import notify                       # lazy: tests never reach the real sender
34	    notify.notify(line)
35

37	def apply(commit, queue_orders_fn, post_fn=None, has_pass_fn=None, marker_fn=None, path=None):
38	    """Apply the verify-PASS for `commit`. `queue_orders_fn(commit)` places the orders (supplied at wire-in).
39	    Returns what it did. Idempotent: a commit already in the applied-set is a no-op."""
40	    path = path or APPLIED
41	    has_pass_fn = has_pass_fn or _has_pass
42	    if not has_pass_fn(commit):
43	        return {"applied": False, "reason": "no verify-PASS on disk for %s" % commit}
44	    if commit in _applied_set(path):
45	        return {"applied": False, "reason": "already applied (idempotent)"}
46	    if marker_fn:
47	        marker_fn(commit)               # optional launch-path proof stamp, supplied at wire-in
48	    queue_orders_fn(commit)             # required: place the orders
49	    (post_fn or _default_post)("PROOF applied — orders queued for %s" % commit)
50	    _record(commit, path)
51	    return {"applied": True, "commit": commit}
52	
53	
54	def _has_pass(commit):
55	    import verifier
56	    return verifier.has_pass(commit)

65	    assert apply("deadbeef", q, post_fn=posts.append, has_pass_fn=lambda c: False, path=p)["applied"] is False
66	    assert queued == [] and posts == []
67	    # PASS present -> applies once
68	    r = apply("cafe123", q, post_fn=posts.append, has_pass_fn=lambda c: True, path=p)
69	    assert r["applied"] is True and queued == ["cafe123"] and len(posts) == 1

72	    assert r2["applied"] is False and queued == ["cafe123"] and len(posts) == 1

101	    """Evaluate at the close and, for each QUEUE decision, write a next-open (EOD-) order that the EXISTING
102	    tested path (evening.process_eod_positions -> paper.simulate_trade at the next open) fills with the exam's
103	    own management (template EXAM_TEMPLATE + the pattern's horizon; manage=trail — all five are trail=false).
104	    HELD signals are logged with the named check, never as a trade. Returns {queued, held, decisions}.
105	
106	    GATED on the R-EXAM-CONFORM proof marker: no order is written until conformance is proven (the fail-safe)."""

123	            })
124	        elif d["verdict"] == "HELD":
125	            held.append(d)
126	    if write and rows:
127	        paper.append_rows(rows)
128	    return {"queued": len(rows), "held": len(held), "proven": proven, "decisions": decisions}

131	def _checklist(LB, token, entry, quotes, bar_date=None):
132	    """The six checks with LIVE inputs where present, else fail-closed. `quotes` carries the live book; a missing
133	    book -> the check that needs it is HELD (named). Reuses LB.checklist (no copy)."""
134	    try:
135	        # exit_price unknown pre-fill: use entry (a zero-move is 'tradeable' unless data is missing); the cost/
136	        # spread/depth checks are what gate a live fill, and they read the stock's own measures + live book.
137	        chk = LB.checklist(int(token), entry, entry, bar_date or _today_str(), *_impact_k(LB),
138	                           spread_scale=1.0, with_impact=True)
139	        return chk if isinstance(chk, dict) else {"verdict": "HELD", "reason": "warm-up — checklist unavailable"}

tool_result: 1	"""R-TWIN-TEST / NIGHT BUNDLE 4 item 6 — the TWIN-RUN HARNESS (code only; the actual run waits for a
2	memory-safe window after 15:45).
3	
4	It replays ONE recorded market day through the real live loop in a REHEARSAL context that provably cannot touch
5	the live ledgers, and compares the result trade-for-trade with the exam engine over the same recording.
6	
7	TWO independent safety layers, so a bug in either still fails closed:
8	  1. PREVENTIVE — the rehearsal routes every order/ledger write to a throwaway temp dir, never a live path.
9	  2. DETECTIVE  — every live ledger is sha256-hashed BEFORE and AFTER; any change is a FAILED-SAFETY verdict
10	                  (louder than a mismatch). A recorded day that never touches a live file is the pass.
11	
12	The live-loop and exam runners are INJECTED (`live_loop_fn`, `exam_fn`) so this module is unit-tested with fakes
13	and spends nothing; the real runners are supplied at run time after 15:45 (they import the live loop + exam and
14	feed the recorded day — that wiring is the run, deliberately not invoked here).
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import glob
19	import hashlib
20	import os
21	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
22	
23	_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
24	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
25	REPORTS = os.path.join(_REPO, "docs", "reports")
26	
27	# Every live ledger that a rehearsal must leave byte-for-byte unchanged.
28	def live_ledgers():
29	    paths = glob.glob(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "*LEDGER*.csv")) + \
30	        glob.glob(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "books", "*.csv"))
31	    return sorted(paths)
32	
33	
34	def hash_ledgers(paths=None):
35	    """{path: sha256 or None if missing}. Never raises."""
36	    out = {}
37	    for p in (paths if paths is not None else live_ledgers()):
38	        try:
39	            out[p] = hashlib.sha256(open(p, "rb").read()).hexdigest()
40	        except Exception:
41	            out[p] = None
42	    return out
43	
44	
45	def changed(before, after):
46	    """The ledger paths whose hash changed between two snapshots (a created or deleted file counts)."""
47	    keys = set(before) | set(after)
48	    return sorted(k for k in keys if before.get(k) != after.get(k))
49	
50	
51	# ---- trade-for-trade comparison (the exam is the reference; a difference in the live loop is the defect) ----
52	_FIELDS = ("signal_time", "verdict", "reason", "fill_price", "exit_time", "exit_reason", "pnl_R")
53	
54	
55	def _key(t):
56	    return (t.get("signal_time"), t.get("stock") or t.get("instrument"))
57	
58	
59	def compare(live_trades, exam_trades):
60	    """MATCH, or a numbered list of every difference (which side is wrong). Aligns on (signal_time, instrument)."""
61	    live = {_key(t): t for t in (live_trades or [])}
62	    exam = {_key(t): t for t in (exam_trades or [])}
63	    diffs = []
64	    for k in sorted(set(live) | set(exam), key=lambda x: tuple("" if v is None else str(v) for v in x)):
65	        lt, et = live.get(k), exam.get(k)
66	        if lt is None:
67	            diffs.append("%s %s: in EXAM only (live loop produced no trade)" % k)
68	            continue
69	        if et is None:
70	            diffs.append("%s %s: in LIVE only (exam produced no trade)" % k)
71	            continue
72	        for f in _FIELDS:
73	            if lt.get(f) != et.get(f):
74	                diffs.append("%s %s: %s live=%r exam=%r" % (k[0], k[1], f, lt.get(f), et.get(f)))
75	    verdict = "MATCH" if not diffs else "DIFF"
76	    return {"verdict": verdict, "differences": [("%d. %s" % (i + 1, d)) for i, d in enumerate(diffs)]}
77	
78	
79	def run(recorded_day, live_loop_fn, exam_fn, out=None):
80	    """Replay one recorded day. `live_loop_fn(recorded_day, rehearsal_dir)` runs the real live loop with all
81	    writes routed to rehearsal_dir; `exam_fn(recorded_day)` runs the exam engine. Both return a list of trade
82	    dicts (fields in _FIELDS + stock/instrument). Returns the verdict record and writes docs/reports/twin-<day>.md.
83	
84	    SAFETY: the live ledgers are hashed before and after; any change makes the verdict FAILED-SAFETY regardless
85	    of the trade comparison."""
86	    import tempfile
87	    rehearsal_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="twin_rehearsal_")
88	    before = hash_ledgers()
89	    live_trades = live_loop_fn(recorded_day, rehearsal_dir)
90	    exam_trades = exam_fn(recorded_day)
91	    after = hash_ledgers()
92	    touched = changed(before, after)
93	    if touched:
94	        rec = {"day": recorded_day, "verdict": "FAILED-SAFETY", "touched_live_ledgers": touched,
95	               "differences": [], "note": "the rehearsal changed a LIVE ledger — the run is void"}
96	    else:
97	        cmp = compare(live_trades, exam_trades)
98	        rec = {"day": recorded_day, "verdict": cmp["verdict"], "touched_live_ledgers": [],
99	               "differences": cmp["differences"],
100	               "counts": {"live": len(live_trades or []), "exam": len(exam_trades or [])}}
101	    _write_report(rec, live_trades, exam_trades, out or os.path.join(REPORTS, "twin-%s.md" % recorded_day))
102	    return rec
103	
104	
105	def _row(t):
106	    return "| " + " | ".join(str(t.get(f, "—")) for f in (("stock",) + _FIELDS)) + " |"
107	
108	
109	def _write_report(rec, live_trades, exam_trades, out):
110	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
111	    head = ("# twin-%s — live loop vs exam, one recorded day\n\n"
112	            "**VERDICT: %s**  ·  live trades %s · exam trades %s  ·  written %s\n\n" % (
113	                rec["day"], rec["verdict"], (rec.get("counts") or {}).get("live", "—"),
114	                (rec.get("counts") or {}).get("exam", "—"), datetime.now(IST).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST")))
115	    if rec["verdict"] == "FAILED-SAFETY":
116	        head += ("The rehearsal changed a LIVE ledger — the run is VOID and this is a defect, not a mismatch:\n" +
117	                 "".join("- %s\n" % p for p in rec["touched_live_ledgers"]))
118	    elif rec["differences"]:
119	        head += "Differences (the exam is the reference; a difference is a defect in the live loop):\n\n" + \
120	                "\n".join(rec["differences"]) + "\n"
121	    else:
122	        head += "MATCH — every trade agrees on signal time, verdict/reason, fill, exit and P&L after charges.\n"
123	    hdr = "| " + " | ".join(("stock",) + _FIELDS) + " |\n|" + "---|" * (1 + len(_FIELDS))
124	    body = ("\n\n## Live loop (rehearsal)\n\n%s\n%s\n\n## Exam engine\n\n%s\n%s\n" % (
125	        hdr, "\n".join(_row(t) for t in (live_trades or [])) or "(none)",
126	        hdr, "\n".join(_row(t) for t in (exam_trades or [])) or "(none)"))
127	    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
128	        f.write(head + body)
129	    return out
130

137	        "pointed at rehearsal_dir (never a reports/ path), and return the trades. The harness hashes the live "

152	               "fill_price": 100.0, "exit_time": "15:20", "exit_reason": "target", "pnl_R": 1.5}]

164	    # 3) DETECTIVE safety: a rehearsal that changes a live ledger is FAILED-SAFETY
165	    import sys

167	    orig = M.live_ledgers
168	    M.live_ledgers = lambda: [led]
169	    try:
170	        def tamper(day, rd):
171	            open(led, "a").write("3,4\n")            # a rehearsal that (wrongly) writes a live ledger

178	    # 4) real runners never run silently
179	    for fn in (real_live_loop, real_exam):

183	        except NotImplementedError:
184	            pass

tool_result: 1	"""NB25 item 63 — the item-4 FULL-CHAIN dry-run harness.
2	
3	Replays one fully-recorded market day through the chain the login launches and writes a CARD to a dry-run log
4	ONLY — never Telegram, never the live ledger:
5	    08:15 self-check line  →  evaluate the day (six checks)  →  for each QUEUE, the order gate decides:
6	      flag PRESENT  → HELD  (order_ok is fail-closed under R-HOLD-AND-FIX)
7	      flag ABSENT   → PLACED to the dry-run log (in a copy — the real flag is never removed)
8	
9	Run twice (flag present / absent) and the two outcomes must be HELD then PLACED — that is the mechanism proof.
10	The INDEPENDENT verifier (scripts/verifier.py, a separate session) rules PASS/FAIL on the log and only then is a
11	PASS marker written; this harness NEVER writes the marker itself (a run does not verify itself). Every heavy dep
12	is injectable, so the logic is unit-tested without the store, the gate, or any ledger.
13	"""
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import datetime
17	import json
18	import os
19	
20	REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
21	DRY_LOG = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "CHAIN-DRY-RUN.log")
22	
23	
24	def _real_evaluate(asof, quotes):
25	    from engine1_rebuild import conform
26	    return conform.evaluate_at_close(asof=asof, quotes=quotes)
27	
28	
29	def _real_order_verdict(flag_present):
30	    """The order-path decision for a queued signal. flag present -> HELD (fail-closed); else the two-key gate."""
31	    from engine1_rebuild import gate
32	    if flag_present and gate.held():
33	        return "HELD"
34	    return "PLACED" if gate.orders_allowed() else "RECORD-ONLY"
35	
36	
37	def _real_0815(status):
38	    from scripts import preopen_line
39	    return preopen_line.line(status)
40	
41	
42	def dry_run(asof, flag_present, status_0815=None, quotes=None,
43	            evaluate_fn=None, order_verdict_fn=None, line_0815_fn=None, log_path=None):
44	    """One pass. Returns {asof, flag_present, ready_line, queued, cards}. Writes the cards to the dry-run log."""
45	    evaluate_fn = evaluate_fn or _real_evaluate
46	    order_verdict_fn = order_verdict_fn or _real_order_verdict
47	    line_0815_fn = line_0815_fn or _real_0815
48	    ready = line_0815_fn(status_0815 or {})
49	    decisions = evaluate_fn(asof, quotes)
50	    queue = [d for d in decisions if d.get("verdict") == "QUEUE"]
51	    verdict = order_verdict_fn(flag_present)
52	    cards = [{"stock": d["stock"], "family": d["family"], "order": verdict} for d in queue]
53	    entry = {"stamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(), "asof": asof, "flag_present": flag_present,
54	             "ready_line": ready, "queued": len(queue), "cards": cards}
55	    with open(log_path or DRY_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:   # dry-run log ONLY — not Telegram, not the ledger
56	        f.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")
57	    return entry
58	
59	
60	def both_passes(asof, status_0815=None, quotes=None, **fns):
61	    """The two required passes. Returns (held_pass, placed_pass, ok) — ok iff flag-present HELD and flag-absent PLACED."""
62	    held = dry_run(asof, True, status_0815, quotes, **fns)
63	    placed = dry_run(asof, False, status_0815, quotes, **fns)
64	    ok = (all(c["order"] == "HELD" for c in held["cards"]) and held["cards"] != [] and
65	          all(c["order"] == "PLACED" for c in placed["cards"]) and placed["cards"] != [])
66	    return held, placed, ok
67

69	def _demo():

71	    log = os.path.join(tempfile.mkdtemp(), "dry.log")
72	    fake_decisions = [{"stock": "AUBANK", "family": "vwap", "verdict": "QUEUE"},
73	                      {"stock": "ETERNAL", "family": "rsi", "verdict": "QUEUE"},
74	                      {"stock": "BSE", "family": "stddev_returns", "verdict": "NO-FIRE"}]
75	    fns = dict(evaluate_fn=lambda a, q: fake_decisions,
76	               order_verdict_fn=lambda flag: "HELD" if flag else "PLACED",
77	               line_0815_fn=lambda s: "✅ READY — 1 cameras up, gate open, 2 orders queued",
78	               log_path=log)
79	    held, placed, ok = both_passes("2026-09-22", status_0815={}, **fns)
80	    assert held["queued"] == 2 and all(c["order"] == "HELD" for c in held["cards"]), held
81	    assert placed["queued"] == 2 and all(c["order"] == "PLACED" for c in placed["cards"]), placed
82	    assert ok is True
83	    # the log got two lines (held pass, placed pass), never Telegram
84	    assert sum(1 for _ in open(log, encoding="utf-8")) == 2
85	    # a chain that does NOT hold with the flag present -> not ok (fail-closed catch)
86	    bad = dict(fns); bad["order_verdict_fn"] = lambda flag: "PLACED"      # places even with the flag -> WRONG
87	    _h, _p, ok2 = both_passes("2026-09-22", status_0815={}, **bad)
88	    assert ok2 is False, "a chain that places under the flag must FAIL the dry run"
89	    print("chain_dry_run demo OK: flag present->HELD, absent->PLACED, cards to log only; places-under-flag FAILS.")

tool_use Read {"file_path": "[redacted]\\Desktop\\trading-engines-v2\\engine1_rebuild\\book20_runner.py", "offset": 360, "limit": 85}
tool_result: 360	def run_once(asof=None, reader=None, write=True, quotes=None):
361	    """One poll of the single order path. Returns a readout. Writes an order ONLY when gate.order_ok holds
362	    for it on this poll; under the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold that is never, so this records the would-be book."""
363	    now_iso = clock.iso()
364	    streak = _live_loss_streak()
365	    cost_frac = money.COST_STATUTORY_RT + 2 * money.half_spread()
366	    cands = _candidates(asof, reader)
367	    by_stock = {c["stock"]: c for c in cands}
368	    # desk rules as CALLERS (R-HOLD-AND-FIX 2): net same-stock fires, and pause on the measured book guard.
369	    fired_orders = [{"stock": c["stock"], "bucket": "daily", "direction": c["direction"], "size": 1.0,
370	                     "strength": abs(c["p_lb"]), "sentence": c["sentence"]} for c in cands]
371	    netted = {int(o["stock"]): o for o in desk.net_orders(fired_orders)} if fired_orders else {}
372	    # R-BOOK20-COMPLETE (3): book guard (measured worst daily DD) + position cap (measured), both fail-closed.
373	    open_now = _open_position_count()
374	    open_keys = _open_pattern_keys()                   # R-FIX-3E (1): (instrument, pattern) already holding a position
375	    block_all = ("*", "*") in open_keys                # fail-closed sentinel: unreadable ledger → no new fires
376	    pos_cap = _measured_position_cap()
377	    cap_paused = open_now >= pos_cap
378	    guard_paused, guard_reason = desk.book_guard(_today_loss_rupees(), _measured_worst_daily_dd())
379	    would_be, written = [], []
380	    for stock, c in list(by_stock.items()):
381	        if stock not in netted:                       # netting cancelled this stock's fires -> no order
382	            continue
383	        c["direction"] = "direct" if netted[stock]["direction"] in ("direct", "long") else "inverse"
384	        tm = _typical_move(c["daily"]["close"] if hasattr(c["daily"], "columns") else c["daily"])
385	        if tm is None:
386	            continue
387	        ok, required = live_20d.required_move_ok(c["p_lb"], cost_frac, tm)
388	        if not ok:
389	            continue
390	        # moment light on the read-only quote (fail-closed offline -> not green -> record-only)
391	        inst = f"NSE:{c['stock']}"
392	        if quotes is not None:
393	            m_ok, _note = desk.moment_light(quotes.get(c["stock"]), key=inst)   # A1: per-stock ceiling
394	        else:
395	            m_ok, _note = live_20d.moment_light_live(inst)
396	        row = {"stock": c["stock"], "direction": c["direction"], "sentence": c["sentence"],
397	               "pattern": c.get("sentence", ""),      # R-FIX-3E (1): the open row carries its pattern for the re-fire key
398	               "required_move": round(required, 5), "typical_move": round(tm, 5),
399	               "moment_green": bool(m_ok), "horizon_days": H}
400	        would_be.append(row)
401	        # R-FIX-3E (1): NO RE-FIRE — never open a second position in a stock this pattern already holds.
402	        refire = block_all or (str(c["stock"]).strip(), str(c.get("sentence", "")).strip()) in open_keys
403	        allowed = (_exam_conform_proven() and (not guard_paused) and (not cap_paused) and (not refire)
404	                   and gate.order_ok(engine=1, moment_green=(m_ok is True), live_loss_streak=streak))
405	        # R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per engine at fire time (PLACED / HELD), once per fire.
406	        _fire_cards(c, c.get("metric", ""), m_ok, bool(write and allowed), refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
407	        if write and allowed:
408	            # R-GO-LIVE-SIMPLE (1): FILL AT THE TOUCH immediately on authorisation — long@ask / short@bid at the
409	            # size available; the would-be mid fill is ledgered beside it. A fault here is ledgered, no order.
410	            # R-FIX-3E (1): trade id is unique per fire (…-<fire ts>), never overwritten.
411	            tid = "BOOK20-%s-%s" % (c["stock"], now_iso)
412	            side = "buy" if c["direction"] in ("direct", "long", "buy") else "sell"
413	            try:
414	                pos = fill_at_touch(tid, c["stock"], side, (quotes or {}).get(c["stock"]),
415	                                    _atr_of(c), H, sentence=c.get("sentence", ""), cost_frac=cost_frac)
416	                if pos:
417	                    pos["metric"] = c.get("metric", "")   # A4: carry the fire's family to the E10/E11 books
418	                    written.append({**row, **pos})
419	                    _entry_message(pos)               # 📈 on every paper entry
420	            except Exception as e:
421	                _fault(tid, "fill_at_touch", e)       # ledgered fault -> no order
422	    if written:
423	        _append_orders(written)
424	    managed = _manage_open_positions(quotes)          # manage_position called per open position (📉 on exit)
425	    filled_this_poll = written
426	    readout = {"mode": gate.mode(), "held": gate.held(), "orders_allowed_now": gate.order_ok(1, moment_green=True),
427	               "live_loss_streak": streak, "candidates": len(cands), "netted_orders": len(netted),
428	               "filled_this_poll": len(filled_this_poll),
429	               "positions_managed": managed, "open_positions": open_now,
430	               "position_cap_measured": pos_cap, "position_cap_paused": cap_paused,
431	               "book_guard_paused": guard_paused, "book_guard_reason": guard_reason,
432	               "would_be_orders": len(would_be),
433	               "orders_written": len(written), "cost_readout": money.cost_readout(),
434	               "note": "single order path (20-day book). Intraday ATR-template retired. "
435	                       "Held until an independent audit PASS (AUDIT-3D): orders_written stays 0."}
436	    try:
437	        with open(READOUT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
438	            json.dump(readout, f, indent=1, default=str)
439	    except Exception:
440	        pass
441	    return readout

## NB39 — unpark with isolation

**The ISOLATION LAW — recorded (CLAUDE.md §27).** The §24 freeze is replaced by isolation, so work unparks without endangering the money chain: **(a)** the Price Engine chain's files, launch path, and process list are off-limits to everything else; **(b)** every other engine is its own **diary-only** process (no orders) until it passes its own audit + smoke check; **(c)** others start only after the 08:15 READY line and only if measured free RAM ≥ the reopen floor + that process's own measured footprint, and are **killed first** under memory pressure (the chain never); **(d)** their failures are **quiet** (✅/⏱ + a diary/held log, never 🙋); **(e)** site work is front-end only. Root swap stays owner-gated.
